  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determining the amount, the Commission is not
  required to consider any potential loss of non-use value relating to a
  public resource that is affected as a result of debris.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing obligation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the authorization must ensure that the
  proof referred to in subsection (1) remains in force for the duration
  of the work or activity in respect of which the authorization is
  issued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of financial responsibility

- **[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for an authorization must provide proof of financial
  responsibility in an amount that is determined by the Commission. The
  proof must be in the form of a letter of credit, guarantee or
  indemnity bond or in any other form satisfactory to the Regulator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing obligation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of an authorization must ensure that the
  proof of financial responsibility remains in force for the duration of
  the work or activity in respect of which the authorization is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of claims

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may require that moneys in an amount of
  not more than the amount prescribed by regulation for any case or
  class of cases, or fixed by the Regulator in the absence of
  regulations, be paid out of the funds available under the letter of
  credit, guarantee or indemnity bond or other proof provided under
  subsection (1) in respect of any claim for which proceedings may be
  instituted under section 302, whether or not those proceedings have
  been instituted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of payment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A required payment must be made in the manner,
  subject to any conditions and procedures, and to or for the benefit of
  the persons or classes of persons that may be prescribed by regulation
  for any case or class of cases, or that may be required by the
  Regulator in the absence of regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a claim is sued for under section 302, there must
  be deducted from any award made pursuant to the action on that claim
  any amount received by the claimant under this section in respect of
  the loss, damage, costs or expenses claimed.

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163802 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of provisions in Part 4

- **[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 272 and 273, subsections
  274(1) and (2), sections 275 and 276, and section 292 in respect of
  those provisions, apply in respect of an offshore power line as if
  each reference in any of those provisions to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an international or interprovincial power line were
    a reference to an offshore power line; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a permit referred to in section 248 or a
    certificate were a reference to an authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection 273(2)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, subsection 273(2) applies only in respect of
  any part of an offshore power line that is in a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of provisions in Part 6

- **[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Part
  6, except sections 315 to 318, 335 and 342, applies in respect of any
  part of an offshore power line that is in a province as if each
  reference in any of those provisions to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company were a reference to the applicant for or
    holder of the authorization issued in respect of the power line;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pipeline or line were a reference to that part of
    the power line; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} hydrocarbons or any other commodity were a
    reference to electricity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection 224(1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 224(1) applies in respect of any part of
  an offshore power line that is in a province as if the reference to
  "any of the circumstances referred to in subsection (2)" were a
  reference to any of the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} leave has been obtained under subsection 272(2)
    or (5) in respect of that part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the authorization in respect of the power line
    contains a condition relating to the utility referred to in
    subsection 224(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the power line has been constructed in
    circumstances specified in an order or regulation made under
    subsection 272(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections 316(1) to
  (3)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 316(1) to (3) apply in respect of any
  part of an offshore power line that is in a province as if each
  reference in any of those provisions to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company were a reference to the holder of the
    authorization issued in respect of the power line; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pipeline or line were a reference to that part of
    the power line.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsections 316(1)
  to (3)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), subsections 316(1) to (3) do
  not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything done under leave obtained under subsection
    272(2) or (5) in respect of any part of an offshore power line that
    is in a province; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any such part that passes on, over, along or under
    a facility if an authorization has been issued in respect of the
    power line and the authorization contains a condition relating to
    that facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of sections 317 and 318

**[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** Sections 317 and 318
apply in respect of an offshore renewable energy project or an offshore
power line as if each reference in that section to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company were a reference to a person; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a pipeline were a reference to an offshore renewable
  energy project or offshore power line.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitations

**[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** The holder of an
authorization must not, unless the Commission has granted leave by
order,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or otherwise transfer to any person its offshore
  renewable energy project or offshore power line, in whole or in part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} purchase or otherwise acquire an offshore renewable
  energy project or offshore power line from any person, in whole or in
  part;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} lease to any person its offshore power line or any
  facility, equipment or system related to its offshore renewable energy
  project, in whole or in part;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} lease from any person an offshore power line --- or
  any facility, equipment or system related to an offshore renewable
  energy project --- other than the one in respect of which the
  authorization is issued, in whole or in part; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the holder is a company, amalgamate with another
  company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry

- **[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may enter on and use any portion of the offshore area in order
  to carry on a work or activity

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorized under subsection 298(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorized under section 101 in respect of an
    abandoned offshore power line or an abandoned facility, equipment or
    system that is in respect of an offshore renewable energy project.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person occupies a portion of the offshore area
  under lawful authority, other than through an authorization, no other
  person is permitted to enter on and use that portion as set out in
  subsection (1) without the consent of the occupier or, if consent has
  been refused, except in accordance with the conditions imposed in an
  arbitration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Study and report

**[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may, by
order, direct the holder of an authorization to study any safety or
environmental protection issue that relates to their offshore renewable
energy project or offshore power line and to report to the Regulator on
the results within the period specified in the order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes section
  297, a condition of an authorization imposed under subsection 298(9),
  an order made under section 310 or a provision of a regulation made
  under section 312 commits an offence and is liable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine of not more
    than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than five
    years, or to both; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine of not more than
    \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one year,
    or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections 379(2) to
  (6)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 379(2) to (6) apply, with any
  modifications that the circumstances require, in respect of an offence
  under this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting works and activities related to offshore
  renewable energy projects and to offshore power lines, for the
  purposes of safety, security and environmental protection;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the conditions referred to in subsection
  298(9);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the introduction into the environment of
  substances, classes of substances and forms of energy in specified
  circumstances;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the creation, updating, conservation and
  disclosure of records;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting arbitrations for the purposes of
  subsection 309(2), including the costs of or incurred in relation to
  such arbitrations; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is to be prescribed under
  this Part, other than the circumstances referred to in subsection
  298(6).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- excluded periods

**[[312.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312.1 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may
make regulations prescribing, for the purposes of subsection 298(6), the
circumstances in which periods may be excluded from the calculation of
the time limit.

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Lands]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1163866 .Part}

### [General Powers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163867 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of company

**[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** A company may, for the
purposes of its undertaking, subject to this Act and to any Special Act
applicable to it,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} enter into and on any Crown land without previous
  licence, or into and on the land of any person, lying in the intended
  route of its pipeline, and make surveys, examinations or other
  necessary arrangements on the land for fixing the site of the pipeline
  and set out and ascertain the parts of the land that are necessary and
  appropriate for the pipeline;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquire, lease and take from any person any land or
  other property necessary for the construction, maintenance, operation
  and abandonment of its pipeline or the maintenance of its abandoned
  pipeline and sell or otherwise dispose of, or lease, any of its land
  or property that has become unnecessary for the purpose of the
  pipeline or the abandoned pipeline;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} construct its pipeline across, on or under the land
  of any person on the located line of the pipeline;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} join its pipeline with the transmission facilities of
  any other person at any point on its route;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} construct, erect and maintain all necessary and
  convenient roads, buildings, houses, stations, depots, wharves, docks
  and other structures and construct, acquire and lease machinery and
  other apparatus necessary for the construction, maintenance, operation
  and abandonment of its pipeline or the maintenance of its abandoned
  pipeline;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} construct, maintain and operate branch lines and for
  that purpose exercise all the powers, privileges and authority
  necessary for that purpose in as full and ample a manner as for a
  pipeline;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} alter, repair or discontinue the works mentioned in
  this section, or any of them, and substitute others in their stead;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} transmit hydrocarbons or any other commodity by
  pipeline and regulate the time and manner in which hydrocarbons or any
  other commodity are to be transmitted and the tolls to be charged; and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} do all other acts necessary for the construction,
  maintenance, operation and abandonment of its pipeline or the
  maintenance of its abandoned pipeline.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Damages and compensation

**[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** A company must, in the
exercise of the powers granted by this Act or a Special Act, do as
little damage as possible, and must make full compensation in the manner
provided in this Act and in a Special Act to all persons interested, for
all damage sustained by them by reason of the exercise of those powers.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exercise of powers outside Canada

**[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** A company operating a
pipeline from a place in Canada to a place on the international boundary
line may exercise, beyond that boundary, to the extent permitted by the
laws there in force, the powers that it may exercise in Canada.

### [Taking and Using Crown Lands]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163883 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Crown lands

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company must not take possession of, use or occupy lands vested in Her
  Majesty without the consent of the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may, with the consent of the Governor in
  Council and on any terms that the Governor in Council may prescribe,
  take and appropriate, for the use of its pipeline and works, the
  portion of the lands of Her Majesty lying on the route of the line
  that have not been granted, conceded or sold, and that is necessary
  for the pipeline, and also the portion of the public beach, or bed of
  a lake, river or stream, or of the lands so vested covered with the
  waters of a lake, river or stream that is necessary for making,
  completing and using its pipeline and works.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation where lands held in trust

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If lands are vested in Her Majesty for a special
  purpose, or subject to a trust, the compensation money that a company
  pays for the lands must be held and applied by the Governor in Council
  for that purpose or trust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply to any of the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything done under leave obtained under subsection
    217(2) or (5) in respect of the pipeline;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any section or part of the pipeline that passes on,
    over, along or under a [[utility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined
    in subsection 217(6), if a certificate has been issued, or an order
    has been made under section 214, in respect of the pipeline and the
    certificate or order contains a condition relating to that utility;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any section or part of the pipeline that passes in,
    on, over, under, through or across a navigable water if a
    certificate has been issued, or an order has been made under section
    214, in respect of the pipeline; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} anything done under any leave obtained under
    section 108 of the [National Energy Board Act](/eng/acts/N-7) at any
    time before July 3, 2013, as that section read from time to time
    before that day.

### [Certain Lands for Which Consent Required]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163897 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent of council of the band

- **[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 35 of the [Indian
  Act](/eng/acts/I-5), a company must not, for the purpose of
  constructing a pipeline or engaging in the activities referred to in
  paragraph 313(a), take possession of, use or occupy lands in a
  [[reserve]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, within the meaning of subsection
  2(1) of the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5), without the consent of the
  [[council of the band]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, within the meaning of
  that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, nothing in subsection (1) is
  to be construed as modifying the application of the other provisions
  of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent of Governor in Council

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company must not take possession of, use or occupy
  the following lands without the consent of the Governor in Council:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[Category IA-N land]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 2(1) of the [Naskapi and the Cree-Naskapi
    Commission Act](/eng/acts/C-45.7);

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} [[Category IA land]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 2(2) of the [Cree Nation of Eeyou Istchee
    Governance Agreement Act](/eng/acts/C-45.75); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[shíshálh lands]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 2(1) of the [shíshálh Nation Self-Government
    Act](/eng/acts/S-6.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the lands referred to in subsection (1) or (3) are
  taken possession of, used or occupied by a company, or if they are
  injuriously affected by the construction of a pipeline, compensation
  must be made by that company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2019, c. 28, s. 10 "317"
- [2018, c. 4, s. 135](#1037606-1197782){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037606-1197782.html"}
- [2022, c. 9, s. 47](#1365947-1368018){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1365947-1368018.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-317-20190828.html "Link to previous version of section 317"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent of Yukon first nation or Governor
in Council

- **[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company must not, if the Yukon first nation concerned does not consent
  to it, take possession of, use or occupy [[settlement
  land]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 2 of the [Yukon
  Surface Rights Board Act](/eng/acts/Y-4.3) without the consent of the
  Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent of Gwich'in Tribal Council or
  Governor in Council

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company must not, if the Gwich'in Tribal Council
  does not consent to it, take possession of, use or occupy Tetlit
  Gwich'in Yukon land without the consent of the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public hearing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If land referred to in subsection (1) or (2) is to be
  taken possession of, used or occupied without the consent of the Yukon
  first nation or Gwich'in Tribal Council, as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a public hearing in respect of the location and
    extent of the land to be taken possession of, used or occupied must
    be held in accordance with the following procedure:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} notice of the date, time and place for the public
      hearing must be given to the Yukon first nation or Gwich'in Tribal
      Council and the public,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on the date and at the time and place fixed for
      the public hearing, an opportunity must be provided for the Yukon
      first nation or Gwich'in Tribal Council and the public to be
      heard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} costs incurred by any party in relation to the
      hearing are at the discretion of the person or body holding the
      hearing and may be awarded on or before the final disposition of
      the issue, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a report on the hearing must be prepared and
      submitted to the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notice of intention to obtain the consent of the
    Governor in Council must be given to the Yukon first nation or
    Gwich'in Tribal Council on completion of the public hearing and
    submission of a report on the hearing to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the lands referred to in subsection (1) or (2) are
  taken possession of, used or occupied by a company, or if they are
  injuriously affected by the construction of a pipeline, compensation
  must be made by that company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[Tetlit Gwich'in Yukon
  land]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[Tetlit Gwich'in Yukon
  land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means land as described in Annex B, as
  amended from time to time, to Appendix C of the Comprehensive Land
  Claim Agreement between Her Majesty in right of Canada and the
  Gwich'in, as represented by the Gwich'in Tribal Council, that was
  approved, given effect and declared valid by the [Gwich'in Land Claim
  Settlement Act](/eng/acts/G-11.8).

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163926 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application restricted

**[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of this
Part to determine compensation matters apply in respect of all damage,
including the loss of value of the lands, caused by the operation of
section 335 or by the pipeline or abandoned pipeline or anything
transmitted by the pipeline but do not apply to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} claims against a company arising out of activities of
  the company unless those activities are directly related to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition or lease of lands for a pipeline or
    abandoned pipeline,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the construction of the pipeline, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the inspection, maintenance or repair of the
    pipeline or abandoned pipeline;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} claims against a company for loss of life or injury
  to the person; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} awards of compensation or agreements respecting
  compensation made or entered into prior to March 1, 1983.

### [Acquisition or Lease of Lands]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163935 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** In sections 321 to 334,
[[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who is entitled to
compensation under section 314.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methods of acquisition or lease

- **[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a company
  may acquire or lease lands for a pipeline under a land acquisition or
  lease agreement entered into between the company and the owner of the
  lands or, in the absence of such an agreement, in accordance with this
  Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company must not acquire or lease lands for a
  pipeline under an agreement referred to in subsection (1) unless the
  agreement includes provision for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} compensation for the acquisition or lease of lands
    to be made, at the option of the owner of the lands, by one lump sum
    payment or by periodic payments of equal or different amounts over a
    specified period of time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} review every five years of the amount of any
    compensation payable in respect of which periodic payments have been
    selected;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} compensation for damages caused by the company's
    operations, pipelines or abandoned pipelines;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} indemnification from all liabilities, damages,
    claims, suits and actions resulting from the company's operations,
    pipelines or abandoned pipelines, other than liabilities, damages,
    claims, suits and actions resulting from

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in Quebec, the gross or intentional fault of the
      owner of the lands, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} elsewhere in Canada, the gross negligence or
      wilful misconduct of the owner of the lands;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} restriction of the use of the lands to the line of
    pipe or other facility for which the lands are, by the agreement,
    specified to be required unless the owner of the lands consents to
    any proposed additional use at the time of the proposed additional
    use;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} compensation to the owner of the lands if the use
    of those lands is restricted by the operation of section 335;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} compensation to the owner of the lands for any
    adverse effect on the remaining lands of the owner, including the
    restriction of their use by the operation of section 335; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any additional terms that are, at the time the
    agreement is entered into, required to be included in it by any
    regulations made under paragraph 333(d).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of proposed acquisition or lease
of lands

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company has determined the lands that may be required for the purposes
  of a section or part of a pipeline, the company must serve a notice on
  all owners of the lands, to the extent that they can be ascertained,
  which notice must set out or be accompanied by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the lands of the owner that are
    required by the company for that section or part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} details of the compensation offered by the company
    for the lands required;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a detailed statement made by the company of the
    value of the lands required in respect of which compensation is
    offered;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedure for approval of the
    detailed route of the pipeline;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedure available under this
    Part in the event that the owner of the lands and the company are
    unable to agree on any matter respecting the compensation payable;
    and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement void or null

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an agreement referred to in section 321 is entered
  into with an owner of lands before a notice is served on the owner
  under this section, that agreement is void or, in Quebec, null.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Abandonment: company's liability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a company serves a notice on an owner of lands
  under subsection (1) and subsequently decides not to acquire or lease
  all or part of the land described in the notice, it is liable to the
  owner for all damages suffered and reasonable costs incurred by the
  owner in consequence of the notice and the abandonment of the
  acquisition or lease of the land.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement of purchase and sale

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** Subsections 321(2) and
322(1) do not apply during any period in which an agreement of purchase
and sale with respect to the lands referred to in those subsections is
in effect between the company and the owner of those lands.

### [Right of Entry]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163968 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immediate right of entry

- **[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 317(1), if the
  Commission considers it appropriate to do so, the Commission may, by
  order, on application to the Regulator in writing by a company, grant
  to the company an immediate right to enter any lands on any conditions
  specified by the Commission in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission is not authorized to make an order
  under subsection (1) unless the Commission is satisfied that the owner
  of the lands in question has, not less than 30 days and not more than
  60 days before the date of the application, been served with a notice
  setting out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of the right of entry referred to in
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the company intends to make its
    application to the Regulator under subsection (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the company intends to enter the
    lands and the period during which the company intends to have access
    to the lands;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the Regulator to which any objection
    in writing that the owner may make concerning the issuance of the
    order may be sent; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the right of the owner to an
    advance of compensation under section 325 if the order is issued and
    the amount of the advance that the company is prepared to make.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance of compensation

**[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** If a company has been
granted an immediate right to enter lands under subsection 324(1), the
owner of the lands is entitled to receive from the company an amount as
an advance of the compensation referred to in subsection 327(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vesting

- **[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order under subsection 324(1) that grants a company an immediate right
  to enter lands is deemed to have vested in the company any title,
  interest or right in the lands that is specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration, recording or filing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within 30 business days after the day on which the
  order is made, the company must register or deposit the order with the
  appropriate land registrar and notify the Regulator and the owner of
  the lands of its deposit.

### [Determination of Compensation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1163986 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determinations regarding compensation

- **[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company and an owner of lands have not agreed on any matter relating
  to the compensation payable under this Part, the Commission, on
  application by a company or any owner, must, by order, determine that
  matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors to consider

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determining any matter under subsection (1) for
  the acquisition or lease of lands, the Commission must consider the
  following factors:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the market value of the lands taken by the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if periodic payments are being made under an
    agreement or an order of the Commission, changes in the market value
    referred to in paragraph (a) since the agreement or order of the
    Commission or since the last review and adjustment of those
    payments, as the case may be;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the loss of use to the owner of the lands taken by
    the company or whose use is otherwise restricted by the operation of
    section 335;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the adverse effect of the taking of the lands by
    the company on the remaining lands of an owner, including by
    restricting their use by the operation of section 335;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the nuisance, inconvenience and noise that may
    reasonably be expected to be caused by or arise from or in
    connection with the operations of the company;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the damage to lands in the area of the lands taken
    by the company that might reasonably be expected to be caused by the
    operations of the company;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} loss of or damage to livestock or other personal
    property or movable affected by the operations of the company;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any special difficulties in relocation of an owner
    or their property;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any other factors that the Commission considers
    appropriate in the circumstances; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} other prescribed factors that are prescribed under
    paragraph 333(e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[market
  value]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (2)(a), [[market
  value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} is the amount that would have been paid
  for the lands if, at the time of their taking, they had been sold in
  the open market by a willing seller to a willing buyer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Settlement land or Tetlit Gwich'in Yukon
land

**[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** If the Commission
determines a compensation matter involving land referred to in section
318, sections 3, 26 to 31, 36, 54 to 58, 63, 67 and 72 of the [Yukon
Surface Rights Board Act](/eng/acts/Y-4.3) apply to the Commission as if
it were the Yukon Surface Rights Board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of compensation payment if land
taken

- **[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Commission makes an award of compensation in favour of a person
  whose lands are taken by a company, the Commission must, by order,
  direct, at the option of that person, that the compensation or any
  part of it be made by one lump sum payment or by periodic payments of
  equal or different amounts over a specified period of time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other awards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission makes an award of compensation in
  favour of any person other than a person referred to in subsection
  (1), the Commission may, by order, direct, at the request of that
  person, that the compensation or any part of it be made by periodic
  payments of equal or different amounts over a specified period of time
  and that there be a periodic review of the compensation or part of the
  compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms to be included in award

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every award of compensation made by the Commission in
  respect of lands acquired or leased by a company must include
  provision for those matters referred to in paragraphs 321(2)(b) to (h)
  that would be required to be included in a land acquisition or lease
  agreement referred to in section 321.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest may be payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, by order, direct a company to pay
  interest on the amount of any compensation awarded by the Commission
  at the lowest rate of interest quoted by banks to the most
  credit-worthy borrowers for prime business loans, as determined and
  published by the Bank of Canada for the month in which, as the case
  may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company entered the lands in respect of which
    the compensation is awarded; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the damages suffered as a result of the operations
    of the company first occurred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period during which interest payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Interest may be awarded under subsection (4) from the
  date the event referred to in paragraph (4)(a) or (b), as the case may
  be, occurred or from any later date that the Commission may specify in
  its award.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

- **[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the amount of compensation awarded to a person by the Commission
  exceeds 85% of the amount of compensation offered by the company, the
  company must pay all legal, appraisal and other costs determined by
  the Commission to have been reasonably incurred by that person in
  asserting that person's claim for compensation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the amount of compensation awarded to a person by
  the Commission does not exceed 85% of the amount of compensation
  offered by the company, the legal, appraisal and other costs incurred
  by that person in asserting their claim for compensation are at the
  discretion of the Commission, and the Commission may, by order, direct
  the company or any other party to the proceedings to pay the whole or
  any part of those costs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions

**[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** The Commission must,
within seven days after it makes a decision on an application, ensure
that a copy of the decision is forwarded by mail to the company and to
each other party to the proceeding.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements supersede Commission decisions

**[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** If, at any time after a
decision of the Commission has been made in respect of lands acquired or
leased by a company, the parties affected enter into a land acquisition
or lease agreement referred to in subsection 321(1), the agreement
supersedes the decision of the Commission.

### [Regulations and Orders Respecting Acquisition, Lease or Taking of Lands and Compensation Matters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164028 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may, with
the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations respecting the
acquisition, lease or taking of lands and compensation matters,
including regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing compensation matters related to the
  operation of section 335;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the form and manner in which a notice is
  served for the purpose of section 201 and subsection 322(1);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the procedures for the acquisition, lease
  or taking of lands for the purpose of subsection 321(1);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing additional terms to be added to those
  that must be included in a land acquisition or lease agreement under
  paragraphs 321(2)(a) to (g);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing additional factors to be added to those
  that the Commission must consider under paragraphs 327(2)(a) to (i);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} governing the procedures for the Commission to make
  orders under section 334;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the receipt and holding by the Regulator
  of copies of any agreements referred to in subsection 321(1) that are
  submitted voluntarily by a company and owner of the lands in question;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing any matter or thing that by this Part is
  to be or may be prescribed; and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} generally to carry out the purposes and provisions of
  this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders of Commission

- **[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application, the Commission may, by order, give directions, including
  to determine any compensation payable under this Part, with respect to
  pipelines and abandoned pipelines in relation to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition, lease or taking of lands;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} lands whose use is restricted by the operation of
    section 335, whether or not the lands were acquired, leased or
    taken; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} damages caused by the activities of the company to
    any person, provincial government, local authority and government or
    Indigenous governing body during their planning, construction,
    operation or abandonment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors to consider

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determinating any compensation matter under
  paragraph (1)(a), the Commission must consider the factors referred to
  in paragraphs 327(2)(a) to (j).

### [Damage Prevention]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164048 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- construction or ground
disturbance

- **[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is prohibited for any person to construct a facility across, on, along
  or under a pipeline or engage in an activity that causes a ground
  disturbance within the prescribed area unless the construction or
  activity is authorized or required by the orders made under
  subsection (3) or (4) or regulations made under subsections (5) or (6)
  and done in accordance with them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- vehicles and mobile
  equipment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for any person to operate a vehicle
  or mobile equipment across a pipeline unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that operation is authorized or required by orders
    made under subsection (3) or (4) or regulations made under
    subsections (5) or (6) and done in accordance with them; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle or equipment is operated within the
    travelled portion of a highway or public road.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, by order, direct the owner of a
  facility constructed across, on, along or under a pipeline in
  contravention of this Act or the Commission's orders or regulations to
  do any things that the Commission considers necessary for the safety
  or security of the pipeline and, if the Commission considers that the
  facility may impair the safety or security of the operation of the
  pipeline, direct the owner to reconstruct, alter or remove the
  facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, by order, give directions

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} governing the design, construction, operation and
    abandonment of facilities constructed across, on, along or under
    pipelines;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the area for the purposes of subsection
    (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the construction of facilities across,
    on, along or under pipelines;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} authorizing ground disturbances within the
    prescribed area;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} governing the measures to be taken in relation to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the construction of facilities across, on, along
      or under pipelines,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the construction of pipelines across, on, along
      or under facilities, other than railways, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} ground disturbances within the prescribed area;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the operation of vehicles or mobile
    equipment across a pipeline and governing the measures to be taken
    in relation to that operation;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} governing the apportionment of costs directly
    incurred as a result of the construction or disturbance authorized
    under this section;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} specifying activities for the purposes of
    paragraph (a) of the definition [[ground
    disturbance]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 in respect of
    pipelines; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} authorizing a company to give the authorization
    referred to in paragraph (c), (d) or (f) on any conditions that the
    company considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may make regulations respecting any
  matter referred to in paragraphs (4)(a) to (f) and (h).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} governing the apportionment of costs directly
    incurred as a result of the construction or disturbance authorized
    under this section; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorizing a company to give the authorization
    referred to in paragraph (4)(c), (d) or (f), subject to any
    conditions that the company considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary prohibition on ground
  disturbances

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Orders made under subsection (4) and regulations made
  under subsection (5) may provide for the prohibition of ground
  disturbances in an area that is situated in the vicinity of a pipeline
  and that may extend beyond the prescribed area during the period that
  starts when a request is made to a pipeline company to locate its
  pipeline and ends

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the end of the third working day after the day
    on which the request is made; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at any later time that is agreed to between the
    pipeline company and the person making the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, by order made subject to any
  conditions that the Commission considers appropriate, exempt any
  person from the application of an order made under subsection (4) or a
  regulation made under subsection (5) or (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes subsection (1) or (2),
  an order made under subsection (3) or (4) or a regulation made under
  subsection (5) or (6) is guilty of an offence and liable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine of not more
    than \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than five
    years or to both; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine of not more than
    \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than one year or
    to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections 379(2) to
  (6)

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 379(2) to (6) apply, with any
  modifications that the circumstances require, to an offence under
  subsection (9).

### [Mines and Minerals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164090 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of mines

**[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** A company must not,
without a designated officer having, by order, authorized it to do so,
locate the line of its proposed pipeline, or construct the pipeline or
any portion of the pipeline, so as to obstruct or interfere with or
injuriously affect the working of or the access or adit to a mine then
open, or for the opening of which preparations are, at the time of the
location, being lawfully and openly made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to minerals

**[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** A company is not,
unless they have been expressly purchased, entitled to mines, ores,
metals, coal, slate, oil, gas or other minerals in or under lands
purchased by it, or taken by it under compulsory powers given to it by
this Act, except only the parts of them that are necessary to be dug,
carried away or used in the construction of the works, and, except as
provided in this section, all those mines and minerals are deemed to be
excepted from the transfer of the lands, unless they have been expressly
included in the transfer documents.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of pipeline from mining
operations

- **[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person must not work or prospect for mines or minerals lying under a
  pipeline or any of the works connected with the pipeline, or within 40
  m of the pipeline, without a designated officer having, by order,
  authorized it to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of oil and gas

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an order from a designated
  officer is not required in the case of a well taking oil or gas from
  lands lying under a pipeline or any of the works connected with the
  pipeline if the well is not drilled within 40 m of the pipeline.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On an application for an order under subsection (1),
  the applicant must submit a plan and profile of the portion of the
  pipeline to be affected, giving all reasonable and necessary
  information respecting the proposed operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The designated officer may grant the application
  referred to in subsection (3), subject to any conditions for the
  protection, safety or security of persons that the designated officer
  considers appropriate, and may, by order, direct that any things be
  done that under the circumstances the designated officer considers to
  be best adapted to remove or diminish the danger arising or likely to
  arise from the proposed operations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of site of mining operations

**[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** If necessary in order
to ascertain whether any mining or prospecting operations have been
carried on so as to injure or be detrimental to a pipeline or its safety
or security or the safety or security of persons, a company may, if a
designated officer, by order, authorizes it to do so and after giving 24
hours notice in writing, enter on any lands through or near which its
pipeline passes where any mining or prospecting operations are being
carried on, and enter into and return from the site of the operations,
and for those purposes the company may make use of any apparatus used in
connection with the operations and use all necessary means for
discovering the distance from its pipeline to the place where the
operations are being carried on.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation for severance, etc., of
mining property

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** A company must, from
time to time, pay to the owner, lessee or occupier of any mines any
compensation that the Commission fixes and, by order, directs to be paid
for or by reason of any severance by a pipeline of the land lying over
the mines, the working of the mines being prevented, stopped or
interrupted, or the mines having to be worked in any manner and under
any restrictions so as not to injure or be detrimental to the pipeline,
and also for any minerals not purchased by the company that cannot be
obtained by reason of the construction and operation of its line.

### [Executions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164108 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assets of company subject to executions

- **[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Act restricts or
  prohibits any of the following transactions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sale under execution of any property of a
    company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the creation of any lien, mortgage, hypothec,
    charge or other security on the property of the company, or of any
    prior claim or right of retention within the meaning of the Civil
    Code of Québec or any other statute of Quebec with respect to
    property of the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the sale, elsewhere than in Quebec, under an order
    of a court of any property of the company to enforce or realize on
    any lien, mortgage, charge or other security on the property of the
    company;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the sale, in Quebec, under an order of a court or
    by judicial authority, of any property of the company to enforce or
    realize on any hypothec, charge or other security on the property of
    the company; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the exercise of remedies for the enforcement and
    realization of any prior claim referred to in paragraph (b) or the
    exercise of any right of retention referred to in that paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of provincial law

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A transaction mentioned in subsection (1) in respect
  of any property of a company is subject to the same laws to which it
  would be subject if the work and undertaking of the company were a
  local work or undertaking in the province in which that property is
  situated.

### [Construing Special Acts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164119 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Construing Special Acts

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided in this Part,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} this Act must be construed as incorporate with a
  Special Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the event of an inconsistency between a provision
  of this Part and of a provision of a Special Act, the provision of the
  Special Act prevails to the extent of the inconsistency.

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Exports and Imports]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1164124 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Oil and Gas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164125 .Subheading}

#### [Prohibition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164126 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- export

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** A person must not
export oil or gas except in accordance with a licence issued under this
Part or as authorized under the regulations made under this Part.

#### [Issuance of Licences for Exportation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164129 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Commission may, with the Minister's approval, issue licences for the
  exportation of oil or gas and impose conditions on them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every licence is subject to the condition that the
  provisions of this Act and its regulations, as well as every order
  made under this Act, will be complied with.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

**[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** The Commission must not
issue a licence unless it is satisfied that the quantity of oil or gas
to be exported does not exceed the surplus remaining after allowance has
been made for the reasonably foreseeable requirements for use in Canada,
having regard to trends in the discovery of oil or gas in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

- **[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission must decide whether to issue a licence for the exportation
  of oil or gas within 180 days after the day on which the Commission
  considers that the applicant has provided a complete application. The
  Commission must make that day public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of jurisdiction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A failure by the Commission to comply with
  subsection (1) within the required time limit does not affect its
  jurisdiction to issue the licence and anything done by it in relation
  to the issuance of that licence remains valid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission requires that the applicant provide
  information or undertake a study with respect to the application and
  the Commission states publicly that this subsection applies, the
  period that is taken by the applicant to comply with the requirement
  is not included in the calculation of the time limit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public notice of excluded period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission must make the day on which the period
  referred to in subsection (3) begins and the day on which it ends
  public as soon as each day is known.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, extend the time limit by
  a maximum of 90 days. The Governor in Council may, on the Minister's
  recommendation, by order, further extend that time limit by any
  additional period or periods of time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial approval

- **[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must decide whether to grant his or her approval of the
  issuance of a licence for the exportation of oil or gas within 90 days
  after the day on which the Commission makes its decision under
  subsection 346(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of jurisdiction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if the Minister grants his or
  her approval after the expiry of the time limit for doing so, the
  Commission's jurisdiction to issue the licence is not affected and
  anything done by it in relation to the issuance of that licence
  remains valid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period for issuing licences

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission must issue the licence within seven
  days after the day on which the Minister grants his or her approval.

#### [Variation, Transfer, Suspension and Revocation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164155 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation of licences

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, on application or its own initiative, vary a licence
  issued under this Division. However, if the Commission considers that
  the variation is neither minor nor technical in nature, the Commission
  must not do so without the approval of the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may approve a variation under
  subsection (1) if he or she considers that it is in the public
  interest to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In varying a licence, the Commission may impose ---
  in addition to or in lieu of any conditions to which the licence was
  previously subject --- any conditions that the Commission considers
  necessary or appropriate to give effect to the purposes and provisions
  of this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of licences

- **[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, on application, transfer a licence issued under this
  Division. However, if the Commission considers that the transfer is
  neither minor nor technical in nature, the Commission must not do so
  without the approval of the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may approve a transfer of a licence if
  he or she considers that it is in the public interest to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In transferring a licence, the Commission may impose
  --- in addition to or in lieu of any conditions to which the licence
  was previously subject --- any conditions that the Commission
  considers necessary or appropriate to give effect to the purposes and
  provisions of this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation of licences ---
application or consent

**[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may, by
order, suspend or revoke a licence issued under this Division if the
holder applies for or consents to the suspension or revocation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation of licences ---
contravention

- **[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, by order, suspend or revoke a licence issued under
  this Division if the holder has contravened a condition of the
  licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation of licences
  --- public interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, by order and with the approval of
  the Minister, suspend or revoke a licence issued under this Division
  if the Commission considers that it is in the public interest to do
  so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to licence holder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission must not make an order under
  subsection (1) or (2) unless the Commission provides the holder of the
  licence with notice of the alleged contravention or the reasons why it
  considers a suspension or revocation to be in the public interest, as
  the case may be, and gives the holder an opportunity to be heard.

#### [Importation of Oil or Gas]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164179 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importer

**[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** Unless exempted by the
regulations, for each prescribed reporting period every person who
imports oil or gas must provide the prescribed information to the
Regulator in the prescribed form and manner.

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164182 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations for the purposes of this
  Division, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the period of validity of licences, the
    quantities that may be exported under licences and any other
    conditions to which licences may be subject;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Commission to make orders
    authorizing the exportation of oil or gas and prescribing the
    circumstances in which the Commission may make, and the conditions
    that may be included in, those orders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions under which the
    exportation of oil or gas may be carried out without a licence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the inspection of any instruments,
    devices, plant, equipment, books, records or accounts or any other
    thing used for or in connection with the exportation or importation
    of oil or gas;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the immediate measures to take in
    respect of oil or gas seized by an officer referred to in section
    380;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, in respect of oil or gas the
    exportation of which is authorized under this Part, or any quality,
    kind or class of that oil or gas or type of service relating to it,
    the price at which or the range of prices within which that oil or
    gas must be sold; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} exempting oil or gas that is exported to Chile or
    Costa Rica, or any quality, kind or class of that oil or gas or type
    of service relating to it, from the application of regulations made
    under paragraph (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The period of validity referred to in paragraph
  (1)(a) begins on a date to be fixed in the licence and must not exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 40 years, in the case of a licence for the
    exportation of natural gas as defined by the regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 25 years, in any other case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Different prices or ranges of prices

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under paragraph (1)(f) may prescribe
  different prices or ranges of prices in respect of different
  countries.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2019, c. 28, s. 10 "353"
- [2020, c. 1, s. 207](#1238415-1253159){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238415-1253159.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-353-20190828.html "Link to previous version of section 353"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Governor in Council
approval

- **[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the
  Governor in Council, the Regulator may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exempting persons or classes of persons from the
    application of section 352; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is to be prescribed under
    that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Regulator

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information to be provided by
    applicants for licences and the procedure to be followed in applying
    for licences and in issuing licences; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing units of measurement and measuring
    instruments or devices to be used in connection with the exportation
    or importation of oil or gas.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Electricity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164208 .Subheading}

#### [Prohibition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164209 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Export

**[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** A person must not
export electricity except in accordance with a permit issued under
section 356 or a licence issued under section 361.

#### [Issuance of Permits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164212 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of an application
  designated by order of the Governor in Council under section 360, the
  Commission must, on application to it and without holding a public
  hearing, issue a permit authorizing the exportation of electricity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must be accompanied by any
  information prescribed by regulation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  applicant must cause a notice of the application to be published in
  the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in any other
  publication that the Commission considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may waive the requirement set out in
  subsection (1) if it considers that there is a critical shortage of
  electricity outside Canada caused by [[terrorist
  activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 83.01(1) of
  the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further information

**[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may,
within a reasonable time after the publication of the notice, require
the applicant to provide any additional information that the Commission
considers necessary to determine whether to make a recommendation under
section 359.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delay of issuance

- **[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may make a recommendation to the Minister, which it must
  make public, that an application for exportation of electricity be
  designated by order of the Governor in Council under section 360 and
  may delay issuing a permit during any period that is necessary for the
  purpose of making such an order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In determining whether to make a recommendation, the
  Commission must seek to avoid the duplication of measures taken in
  respect of the exportation by the applicant and the government of the
  province from which the electricity is to be exported and must take
  into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effect of the exportation of the electricity on
    provinces other than that from which the electricity is to be
    exported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether the applicant has

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} informed those who have declared an interest in
      buying electricity for consumption in Canada of the quantities and
      classes of service available for sale, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} given an opportunity to buy electricity on
      conditions as favourable as the conditions specified in the
      application to those who, within a reasonable time after being so
      informed, demonstrate an intention to buy electricity for
      consumption in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any factors that are specified in the regulations.

#### [Orders]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164235 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where licence required

- **[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make orders

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating an application for exportation of
    electricity as an application in respect of which section 361
    applies; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} revoking any permit issued in respect of the
    exportation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No order may be made under subsection (1) more than
  45 days after the day on which a permit is issued in respect of the
  application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an order is made under subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a permit must not be issued in respect of the
    application; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any application in respect of the exportation must
    be dealt with as an application for a licence.

#### [Issuance of Licences]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164247 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, subject to the approval of the Governor in Council,
  issue a licence for the exportation of electricity in relation to
  which an order made under section 360 is in force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a licence, the Commission must take
  into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effect of the exportation of the electricity on
    provinces other than from which the electricity is to be exported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether the applicant has

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} informed those who have declared an interest in
      buying electricity for consumption in Canada of the quantities and
      classes of service available for sale, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} given an opportunity to buy electricity on
      conditions as favourable as the conditions specified in the
      application to those who, within a reasonable time after being so
      informed, demonstrate an intention to buy electricity for
      consumption in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any factors that are specified in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any permit issued in respect of an application for a
  permit for the exportation of electricity in relation to which an
  order made under section 360 is in force and that is not revoked by
  the order is revoked on the Commission's deciding not to issue a
  licence for that exportation.

#### [Conditions of Permits and Licences]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164260 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions --- permits

- **[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may make a permit issued under section 356 subject to any
  conditions respecting the matters prescribed by the regulations that
  it considers necessary or in the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions --- licences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may make a licence issued under
  section 361 subject to any conditions that it considers appropriate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** Every permit and
licence is subject to the condition that the provisions of this Act and
its regulations, as well as every order made under this Act, will be
complied with.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** The period of validity
of a permit or licence must not exceed 30 years.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation or transfer of permits or
licences

- **[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, on application or its own initiative, vary a permit or
  licence issued in respect of the exportation of electricity and may,
  on application, transfer such a permit or licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In varying or transferring a permit or licence, the
  Commission may impose --- in addition to or in lieu of any conditions
  to which the permit or licence was previously subject --- any
  conditions that the Commission considers necessary or appropriate in
  order to give effect to the purposes and provisions of this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation of permits or
licences

- **[[366]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, by order, suspend or revoke a permit or licence issued
  in respect of the exportation of electricity if the holder applies for
  or consents to the revocation or suspension or the holder has
  contravened a condition of the permit or licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission must not make an order under
  subsection (1) for a contravention unless the Commission provides the
  holder of the permit or licence with notice of the alleged
  contravention and gives the holder an opportunity to be heard.

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164280 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Governor in Council

- **[[367]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-367 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations for the purposes of this
  Division, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing matters in respect of which conditions
    may be imposed on permits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying factors that the Commission must take
    into account in deciding whether to recommend to the Minister that
    an application for a permit for the exportation of electricity be
    designated by order of the Governor in Council under section 360;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} specifying factors that the Commission must take
    into account in deciding whether to issue a licence for the
    exportation of electricity in relation to which an order made under
    section 360 is in force; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the inspection of any instruments, devices, plant,
    equipment, books, records or accounts or any other thing used for or
    in connection with the exportation of electricity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Regulator

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may make regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information to be provided with applications
    for permits for the exportation of electricity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} units of measurement and measuring instruments or
    devices to be used in connection with the exportation of
    electricity.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Interprovincial Oil and Gas Trade]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164292 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164293 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[designated area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[designated area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the following,
    as referred to in an order made under subsection 369(1),

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a province;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Sable Island; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any area of land, not within a province, that
      belongs to Her Majesty in right of Canada or in respect of which
      Her Majesty in right of Canada has the right to dispose of or
      exploit the natural resources and that is situated in submarine
      areas in the internal waters of Canada, the territorial sea of
      Canada or the continental shelf of Canada. ([région
      désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[designated oil or gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[designated oil or gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means oil or gas, or
    both, or any quality or kind of oil or gas, or both, that is the
    subject of an order made under subsection 369(1). ([pétrole ou gaz
    désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[movement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[movement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of oil or gas, excludes
    an exportation. ([acheminement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

#### [Control by Regulator]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164302 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order in Council

- **[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, by order, give the supervision and control of
  the movement of designated oil or gas out of a designated area to the
  Regulator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} While an order made under subsection (1) remains in
  force, a person must not move designated oil or gas out of the
  designated area except in accordance with a licence issued under this
  Division or as authorized under the regulations made under this
  Division.

#### [Issuance of Licences]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164308 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance

- **[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Commission may, on any conditions that it may impose, issue a licence
  to any person permitting the movement of designated oil or gas out of
  the designated area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every licence is subject to the conditions that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of this Act and its regulations, as
    well as every order made under this Act, will be complied with; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of the [Energy Administration
    Act](/eng/acts/E-6) and its regulations that are applicable to the
    designated oil or gas in respect of which the licence is issued will
    be complied with.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

- **[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before issuing a licence, the
  Commission must take into account all factors that it considers
  relevant, including the equitable distribution of designated oil or
  gas in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation, transfer, suspension and
  revocation of licences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 348 to 351 apply in respect of a licence
  issued under this Division.

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1164321 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Governor in Council

- **[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations for the purposes of this
  Division, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the period of validity of licences, the
    approval required for the issuance of licences, the quantities that
    may be moved out of the designated area under the authority of a
    licence and any other conditions to which licences may be subject;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the inspection of any instruments,
    devices, plant, equipment, books, records or accounts or any other
    thing used for or in connection with the movement of designated oil
    or gas out of the designated area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- Regulator

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may make regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information to be provided by applicants for
    licences and the procedure to be followed in applying for and
    issuing licences; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} units of measurement and measuring instruments or
    devices to be used in connection with the movement of designated oil
    or gas out of the designated area.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Implementation of Free Trade Agreements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1164331 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
    the [Canada-Chile Free Trade Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-1.6). ([ALÉCC)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}

[[CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
    the Canada --- Costa Rica Free Trade Agreement Implementation
    Act. ([ALÉCCR]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
    definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
    [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65). ([ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[energy goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[energy goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any goods for the
    exportation of which a licence or permit issued under this Part or
    an order made under the regulations is required. ([produits
    énergétiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
    208\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2019, c. 28, s. 10 "373"
- [2020, c. 1, s. 208](#1238417-1253164){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238417-1253164.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-373-20190828.html "Link to previous version of section 373"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principle
